LEIT AVATAR, STYLE B: PIXEL MAPS (round 4)

Grid: 64 x 80. Head centre (31,29), eye centres (24,34) and (39,34), neck (32,48) in the standing pose.
Map walker: 16 x 22, four frames.
Two characters per pixel, so tones fit the brief's notation. '. ' is empty.

Slots: S skin, H hair and brows, E eyes, C cheeks, T top, B bottoms, F shoes, G glasses frame (new), A hat (new).
Tones, written per pixel:
  base        uppercase letter          H
  shade       lowercase letter          h
  deep shade  lowercase letter + dot    h.
  highlight   round 2 symbols           + skin  * hair  ~ eyes  ^ cheeks  = top  - bottoms  _ shoes  ! glasses  ? hat
Fixed colours (whites of the eyes, pupils, metal, items) are digits and the letters i-z, listed under each map.

Maps are generated from avatar-draw.js, which is the source of truth. Layers are shown in default colours
with the outline rule already applied (deep on edges against empty space, shade against lower layers),
exactly as the engine composites them. See README.md for the tone rules.

######## HAIR
